	texture = "gfx/interface/icons/production_method_icons/modern_future/automation_xvii.png"
	state_modifiers = {
		workforce_scaled = {
			state_pollution_generation_add = 34
		}
	}
	building_modifiers = {
		workforce_scaled = {
			goods_input_communication_services_add = 8.5
			goods_input_electricity_add = 8.5
			goods_input_engines_add = 8.5
			goods_input_software_add = 6
		}
		level_scaled = {
			building_employment_machinists_add = 300
			building_employment_engineers_add = 700
		}
		unscaled = {
			goods_output_industrial_robots_mult = 0.4
		}
	}
	unlocking_technologies = { tech_manufacturing_17 }
}
